These results demonstrate that complementary multimodal information can improve recognition performance without requiring a large model ensemble.

</details>


### [37] [Pretraining Multiple Instance Learning Networks with Multi-Teacher Distillation from Pathology Slide Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14703)
*Mingxi Fu,Jiawen Li,Renao Yan,Jiali Hu,Qiehe Sun,Tian Guan,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蒸馏的MIL预训练框架，利用两个WSI基础模型（TITAN和CARE）作为教师，将表征知识迁移到多种轻量级MIL架构中，并引入角分散归一化蒸馏损失平衡多教师监督。在15个基准数据集上的实验表明，该预训练方法在linear probing和few-shot场景下显著优于从头训练，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL聚合器通常为每个下游任务从头训练，仅依赖有限的slide-level标签同时学习聚合机制和判别表示，导致优化不稳定、过拟合和迁移性差。虽然自然图像领域有预训练的ResNet和ViT，但MIL缺乏高质量的slide-level预训练数据，且MIL模型轻量、弱监督，大规模预训练困难。

Method: 提出蒸馏预训练框架：(1) 利用TITAN和CARE两个slide-level基础模型作为教师；(2) 设计角分散归一化（Angular Dispersion Normalized）蒸馏损失，平衡不同教师的监督；(3) 将蒸馏得到的权重作为下游任务的初始化，支持linear probing和全参数微调。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上系统评估，预训练在linear probing和few-shot场景下显著优于从头训练，全参数微调也有提升，同时保持轻量MIL模型的计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出的MIL预训练框架能有效提升MIL聚合器的性能、稳定性和迁移性，为MIL提供了一种可复用的预训练初始化的解决方案。

Abstract: Multiple instance learning (MIL) has become the main paradigm for whole-slide image (WSI) analysis in computational pathology. However, existing MIL aggregators are still typically trained from scratch for each downstream task, relying on limited slide-level labels to learn both aggregation mechanisms and downstream discriminative representations simultaneously. As a result, they often suffer from unstable optimization, overfitting, and limited transferability. Similar to pretrained ResNet and Vision Transformer models in natural image learning, MIL also requires reusable pretrained initialization. However, high-quality slide-level pretraining data remain scarce, and MIL models are usually lightweight and weakly supervised, making large-scale pretraining difficult in practice. To address this challenge, we propose a distillation-based pretraining framework for MIL, which leverages two slide-level foundation models, TITAN and CARE, as teachers to transfer their representational knowledge into a diverse set of MIL architectures. To effectively balance supervision from different teachers, we further introduce an angular dispersion normalized distillation loss. The distilled weights are then used as initialization for downstream adaptation. We conduct systematic evaluations on 15 benchmark datasets under both linear probing and full-parameter fine-tuning, and further validate its advantages in few-shot scenarios. Experimental results show that pretraining generally improves MIL aggregators over from scratch training, especially in linear-probing and few-shot settings, while maintaining the computational efficiency of lightweight MIL models. Code is available at https://github.com/fu0201/MIL_Pretrained.

</details>


### [38] [Variational Inference for Bird's Eye View Segmentation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14710)
*Jingyue Shi,Huaicheng Li,Junhui Zhao,Yanxiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于变分流变换网络（TVB）的BEV分割方法，利用CVAE骨干网络、归一化流和BEV注意力融合模块，实现多相机到统一BEV地图的隐式学习，在nuScenes和OPV2V数据集上取得先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV感知是自动驾驶的关键技术，但多相机数据融合及复杂外部环境下的有效感知仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 将BEV分割问题重构为变分推理框架；提出TVB网络，以CVAE为骨干生成多个BEV候选图；引入归一化流增强生成分布的表达能力；设计BEV注意力融合（BAF）模块自适应集成多个候选结果。

Result: 在nuScenes和OPV2V数据集上，所提方法在多相机BEV分割和车道环境感知任务中均取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 变分推理框架与归一化流结合的BEV分割范式有效提升了多相机融合感知的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The bird's eye view (BEV) has emerged as a pivotal approach for environmental perception in autonomous driving, providing a unified spatial representation for vehicles. Nevertheless, despite BEV's significance in addressing the challenges inherent to autonomous driving, effectively fusing data from multiple camera sensors and operating in complex external driving environments remains a considerable challenge. To mitigate this issue, we recast the BEV segmentation problem within a variational inference framework. In this paper, we propose a novel transformer-based variational flow transformation network for BEV segmentation, denoted as TVB. Our architecture implicitly learns the mapping from multiple camera views to a unified canonical BEV map during training by exploiting posterior BEV supervision. TVB employs a conditional variational auto encoder (CVAE) as its backbone and produces multiple BEV map candidates. To augment the realism of the generated BEV maps, we integrate normalizing flows into the map generation process, enabling the construction of more complex and expressive probability distributions. Furthermore, we design a BEV-attention fusion (BAF) module that harnesses attention mechanisms to adaptively integrate the multiple candidate BEV maps. Experimental results, evaluated on both the nuScenes and OPV2Vdatasets, demonstrate that our proposed method achieves superior performance in multi-camera view BEV segmentation and lane environment perception.

</details>


### [39] [VideoSEMA: a scalable and efficient Mamba-like attention for video understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14711)
*Nhat Thanh Tran,Fanghui Xue andShuai Zhang,Jiancheng Lyu,Yunling Zheng,Yingyong Qi,Jack Xin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoSEMA模型，采用分体时空注意力（空间上使用类似Mamba的SEMA块，时间上使用softmax注意力），在视频分类任务中超越ViT和Mamba模型，且在高分辨率下退化更平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型（Vision Transformer和Mamba）计算成本高，且在分辨率扩展时性能下降明显；作者希望设计一种更高效且可扩展的时空注意力机制。

Method: 提出VideoSEMA，空间上使用SEMA块（局部窗口注意力+全局平均池化的Mamba-like宏架构），时间上使用softmax注意力；理论上证明了在特定秩条件下，分体时空注意力等价于全局时空注意力。

Result: 在K400上超越更重的ViT和Mamba模型；在SSv2上，同参数量下达到最高top-1准确率；从224²扩展到1024²分辨率时，精度退化远优于VideoMamba。

Conclusion: VideoSEMA是一种高效、可扩展的视频分类模型，可拓展到更长视频（通过膨胀/稀疏时间注意力）。

Abstract: We present for video understanding (classification) a split space-time attention model, VideoSEMA, consisting of a scalable and efficient Mamba-like attention (SEMA) block in space and a softmax temporal attention in time. In each frame, SEMA attention applies a local window attention in parallel with a global averaging in a Mamba macro-architecture, which is called Mamba-like. Under certain rank conditions, we prove that the computationally cheaper split space-time attention is equivalent to full space-time attention. On benchmark K400 data sets, VideoSEMA out-performs heavier vision transformer and Mamba models. On benchmark SSv2 data, VideoSEMA leads in top-1 accuracy among models of similar parameter sizes. As image resolution scales up from standard $224^2$ to $1024^2$ on K400 and without fine-tuning, VideoSEMA degrades much more gracefully than VideoMamba in accuracy. It is promising to extend VideoSEMA to longer videos with a dilated/sparse temporal attention.

</details>


### [40] [Causal-Adversarial Probing of Clinical Covariates for Prostate MRI Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14720)
*Yipei Wang,Shiqi Huang,Wen Yan,Weixi Yi,Dean C. Barratt,Mark Emberton,Daniel C. Alexander,Veeru Kasivisvanathan,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果推理框架，通过对抗性抑制方法分析前列腺MRI癌症分级模型中临床协变量的作用，区分有害的捷径信息与有用的疾病相关信号。在2903例患者数据上验证，发现抑制年龄、BMI、饮酒量可提升模型泛化性能，而抑制PSA和前列腺体积则会降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在前列腺MRI癌症分级中可能编码临床协变量，这些变量要么反映有用的疾病信号，要么作为非泛化性的捷径信息，但其作用通常被假设而非被验证。本文旨在系统地区分这两类协变量。

Method: 基于因果推理框架，将mpMRI外观和ISUP分级建模为潜在肿瘤病理的观察变量，而非直接将MRI视为分级的直接原因。采用对抗性框架，在保持MRI分级预测能力的同时，逐次抑制单个临床协变量的可解码性，从而测试各变量是 nuisance、proxy 还是 irrelevant 协变量。

Result: 在2903例前列腺MRI检查上开发验证，并在576例外部数据上验证。二分类ISUP Grade Group ≥2任务中，抑制年龄、BMI、饮酒量分别提升AUC 1.23%、0.84%、1.42%（p<0.05）；抑制PSA和前列腺体积则降低AUC 1.91%和7.61%（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 对抗性协变量抑制可作为实用的表示层面分析方法，用于区分前列腺MRI分级模型中潜在有害的依赖关系与有用的信息信号。

Abstract: Deep learning models for prostate MRI-based cancer grading may encode clinical covariates that either reflect useful disease-related signal or non-generalising shortcut information, but their role is usually assumed. We propose a causal-reasoning framework for probing covariate dependence in MRI-based International Society of Urological Pathology (ISUP) Grade Group prediction. Rather than treating mpMRI as a direct cause of grade, we model MRI appearance and ISUP grade as observations of latent tumour pathology, and test whether candidate clinical variables act as nuisance correlates, disease-related proxies, or irrelevant covariates in the learned representation. We implement this using an adversarial framework that suppresses the decodability of individual clinical covariate at a time while preserving MRI-based grade prediction. The approach is developed and evaluated on 2,903 prostate MRI examinations, with external validation on 576 patients. We report a set of interesting and previously under-explored imaging-to-clinical-variable interactions in the context of deep learning generalisation. For examples, in binary ISUP Grade Group $\geq2$ classification, suppressing age, BMI, and alcohol use improved AUC by 1.23%, 0.84%, and 1.42%, respectively (all p < 0.05), suggesting reduced non-generalising covariate information; In contrast, suppressing PSA and prostate volume degraded AUC by 1.91% and 7.61% (all p < 0.001), indicating that these variables carried task-relevant signal. These findings show that adversarial covariate suppression can provide a practical representation-level analysis for distinguishing potentially harmful dependence from informative signal in prostate MRI grading models.

</details>


### [41] [Multimodality as Supervision: Self-Supervised Specialization to the Test Environment via Multimodality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14721)
*Kunal Pratap Singh,Ali Garjani,Rishubh Singh,Muhammad Uzair Khattak,Efe Tarhan,Jason Toskov,Andrei Atanov,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Amir Zamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Test-Space Training (TST)方法，通过在测试环境中收集多模态数据进行自监督预训练，实现无需大规模互联网数据即可获得与DINOv2、CLIP等通用模型竞争的性能，并探讨了数据与模态之间的替代关系以及模型专业化与泛化能力的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在受限测试环境下，利用设备丰富的多模态传感器通过跨模态学习进行自监督预训练的可能性，以期减少对外部大规模互联网数据集的依赖，受发展心理学中生物 agents 利用多模态构建环境表征的启发。

Method: 提出Test-Space Training (TST)框架：在特定测试环境中收集多模态数据，并在该数据上进行自监督预训练（跨模态学习），然后在同一环境的下游任务上评估模型性能。

Result: 仅使用测试环境收集的多模态数据，通过跨模态学习即可获得与DINOv2、CLIP等在大规模互联网数据集上预训练的通用模型相竞争的性能；揭示了数据与模态之间的替代关系，以及预训练数据量在模型专业化与泛化能力之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 提出了替代大规模互联网数据预训练的新范式，即通过在测试环境中收集丰富的多模态数据进行自监督学习；同时发现模态多样性可部分替代数据规模，且模型可在专业化与泛化之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Cross-modal learning, i.e., learning to predict one modality from another, is a fundamental mechanism for self-supervision via leveraging multimodality. Many practical applications, e.g., deploying a household robot, involve devices that are equipped with a rich set of sensors that enable multimodal sensing in their test environment. This presents an opportunity to apply cross-modal learning to the multimodal data sensed by these devices to learn representations. Findings in developmental psychology also suggest that biological agents leverage it to build an effective representation of their surroundings.
  To study this, we propose a controlled setup, where we restrict a user device to just a given test environment. It results in a specialization setup where we attempt to develop a performant model for this specific test environment. Under this setup, we develop Test-Space Training (TST), which performs multimodal data collection in the test environment and performs self-supervised pre-training on it. We evaluate these models on various downstream tasks in the same environment.
  Under this setup, we find various interesting insights, such as collecting rich multimodal data only from the test environment and leveraging cross-modal learning, we can achieve competitive results with generalist models (e.g., DINOv2 and CLIP) pre-trained on large-scale internet datasets. This enables an alternative scenario where the need for external Internet-scale datasets for pre-training models is reduced. We also present a set of analyses and ablations that raise intriguing points on substituting data with (multi)modality, and how varying pre-training data enables a tradeoff between a model's abilities to specialise to a test environment, and generalize to held-out spaces.

</details>


### [42] [AE-UAV: An Air-to-Air Event-Based UAV Tracking Benchmark and a Real-Time Frequency-Domain Tracker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14726)
*Zixin Jiang,Bing He,Chaoran Xiong,Zhenzhen Wang,Xin Zhao,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AE-UAV——首个空中拍摄的空对空事件相机无人机跟踪基准数据集，包含178条飞行序列和连续时间三次B样条标注；同时提出轻量级无训练的频率域跟踪方法FSFT，在仅CPU平台上达到420 FPS，保留93.97%的GPU方法精度，实现5.32倍有效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机在低照度、过曝光和高速运动下性能严重退化；事件相机虽有高时间分辨率和动态范围优势，但缺乏专用的空对空事件相机数据集，且现有跟踪器高度依赖GPU加速和大规模训练数据，难以部署在资源受限的无人机平台上。

Method: 构建AE-UAV数据集（178条飞行序列，连续时间三次B样条标注）；提出FSFT方法，将频率域模板匹配与搜索区域预测及基于检测的漂移校正无缝集成，为无训练、轻量级框架，可在纯CPU上运行。

Result: FSFT在仅CPU硬件上达到420 FPS，保持GPU依赖方法93.97%的精度，实现5.32倍有效加速，并展现出优越的时间分辨率泛化能力。

Conclusion: AE-UAV数据集和FSFT方法为空中遥感目标跟踪提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案，填补了空对空事件相机跟踪领域的空白。

Abstract: Air-to-air (A2A) unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) tracking is fundamental to airborne remote sensing of low-altitude aerial targets. However, the deployment of continuous, real-time tracking systems on UAVs presents significant challenges. In A2A scenarios, traditional frame-based cameras suffer from severe performance degradation under low illumination, overexposure, and high-speed motion owing to their limited dynamic range and fixed temporal sampling. Although event cameras offer a promising alternative with microsecond temporal resolution and a high dynamic range, current research is bottlenecked by two primary issues: 1) the absence of dedicated A2A event-based datasets, and 2) the heavy reliance of existing trackers on GPU acceleration and extensive training data, rendering them impractical for resource-constrained UAVs. To bridge these gaps, we introduce AE-UAV, an air-to-air event-based UAV tracking benchmark. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first airborne-captured event camera dataset for A2A tracking, comprising 178 flight sequences with continuous-time cubic B-spline annotations. Furthermore, we propose the Fast-Slow Frequency-domain Tracking (FSFT) method. This lightweight, training-free framework seamlessly integrates frequency-domain template matching with search region prediction and detection-based drift correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FSFT operates at an ultra-fast 420 frames per second (FPS) on CPU-only hardware. It retains 93.97% of the accuracy of state-of-the-art GPU-dependent methods while delivering a 5.32-fold effective speedup and exhibiting superior temporal resolution generalization, thereby providing a highly efficient and robust solution for airborne remote sensing of aerial targets. The dataset and source code are available at https://github.com/MSP-xEN/AE-UAV.

</details>


### [43] [WorkDrive: Roadwork Chain of Causation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14727)
*Tianyi Jiang,Wen Zhang,Sihan Yang,Ming Lu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorkDrive是一个面向自动驾驶视觉语言模型在道路施工区域（work-zone）的框架，通过构建感知驱动的因果推理（Chain-of-Causation）并结合强化学习（GRPO）对齐轨迹预测，在ROADWork数据集上实现了轨迹平均位移误差（ADE）降低9.0%+3.0%的渐进式改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶VLM在道路施工区域表现不佳，因为施工区域缺少车道线、永久标牌等熟悉视觉线索，仅靠临时装置（锥桶、护栏）重新定义可行驶区域；VLM虽能检测这些物体，但缺乏显式引导时仍会锚定预训练中的熟悉元素，无法将施工区域观察结果与正确的规划决策关联起来。

Method: 提出WorkDrive框架：(1) 自动化多任务感知pipeline提取结构化场景事实，注入Chain-of-Causation (CoC)标注pipeline，将标注器注意力重定向到领域特定元素；(2) 用生成的推理标签进行监督微调(SFT)；(3) 采用基于一致性奖励（横向元动作与预测轨迹之间的一致性）的GRPO强化学习。

Result: 在最大公开施工区域数据集ROADWork上：提出的roadwork CoC使轨迹平均位移误差(ADE)降低9.0%，基于一致性的GRPO进一步降低3.0%，实现相对于纯轨迹基线的渐进式改进。

Conclusion: 通过感知驱动的因果推理链将施工区域领域知识注入VLM，结合一致性强化学习，能有效提升自动驾驶在施工场景下的轨迹预测精度。

Abstract: Autonomous driving vision-language models (VLMs) struggle in roadwork zones, where familiar visual cues such as lane markings and permanent signs are altered or absent, and temporary devices such as cones and barriers redefine the drivable corridor. VLMs can detect these objects, but without explicit guidance they anchor their reasoning on familiar elements from pre-training and fail to connect work-zone observations to correct planning decisions. We propose WorkDrive, a framework that constructs perception-grounded causal reasoning for work zones and aligns it with trajectory prediction. An automated multitask perception pipeline extracts structured scene facts and injects them into a Chain-of-Causation (CoC) annotation pipeline, redirecting the annotator's attention to domain-specific elements. The resulting reasoning labels are used for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with a single reward: consistency between lateral meta-actions and the predicted trajectory. On ROADWork, the largest public work-zone dataset, the proposed roadwork CoC reduces trajectory average displacement error (ADE) by 9.0\%, and consistency-based GRPO yields a further 3.0\%, achieving progressive improvement over the trajectory-only baseline. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [44] [VQ-Touch: A Data-Efficient Tactile Generation Framework Across Sensors and Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14728)
*Kailin Lyu,Long Xiao,Jianing Zeng,Di Wu,Lin Shu,Jie Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VQ-Touch是一个支持跨传感器和多场景的触觉图像生成框架，通过DM-VQGAN高效提取触觉特征，并结合离散扩散解码器与统一条件接口，在少样本混合训练下实现主流传感器兼容，性能超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉图像生成方法依赖特定传感器的大规模多样化数据集，数据利用效率低、泛化能力不足，且在视觉受限环境下表现不佳，亟需一种高效且鲁棒的跨传感器触觉生成方案。

Method: 提出VQ-Touch框架，包含：(1) DM-VQGAN作为高效的触觉表示学习器，提取复杂形变和纹理特征；(2) 离散扩散解码器结合统一条件接口，支持图像和标签等多模态生成任务；(3) 通过少样本混合训练增强模型泛化能力，实现与主流传感器及其变体的兼容。

Result: VQ-Touch在多项任务中超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: VQ-Touch通过高效的触觉表示学习和统一条件建模，成功实现了跨传感器、多场景的触觉图像生成，为机器人感知和人机交互提供了更实用的触觉信息获取方案。

Abstract: Tactile image generation significantly reduces the dependency on expensive and wear-prone sensors by synthesizing high-fidelity tactile data, offering an efficient solution for tactile information acquisition in robotic perception and human-machine interaction systems. However, existing methods depend on large-scale, diverse datasets from specific sensors and lack efficient data utilization and robust generalization capabilities, struggling in vision-limited environments. To address this, we introduce VQ-Touch, a tactile generation framework that supports both cross-sensor and multi-scenario applications. Specifically, to efficiently extract complex deformation and texture features from the data, we propose DM-VQGAN, an effective tactile representation learner. Furthermore, we introduce a discrete diffusion decoder with a unified conditioning interface, supporting multimodal generation tasks such as images and labels, and enhances the model's generalization capability through few-shot mixed training, thus achieving compatibility with current mainstream sensors and their variants. Experiments show that VQ-Touch surpasses state-of-the-art methods in multiple tasks.

</details>


### [45] [GeoDetect: Geometric Adversarial Detection for VLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14737)
*Afsaneh Hasanebrahimi,Hanxun Huang,Christopher Leckie,James Bailey,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言预训练模型（VLPs）嵌入空间的几何结构，发现其存在不同于单模态视觉模型的各向异性。基于此，提出GeoDetect方法，利用几何分数检测对抗样本，在多种VLP架构和攻击设置下均表现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练模型（VLPs）虽广泛应用，但仍易受对抗攻击。尽管对抗检测方法在单模态（视觉或语言）中已取得成功，但在多模态模型中的有效性和可靠性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 首先分析VLP嵌入空间的几何结构，发现其存在与单模态视觉模型不同的结构化各向异性。理论分析表明，在此各向异性结构下，对抗攻击会增加干净样本与对抗样本（AEs）之间的期望几何分离。具体而言，对抗样本与随机采样点的期望距离始终大于干净样本，表明对抗样本倾向于将表示推出流形区域。基于此，提出GeoDetect方法，利用几何分数衡量这种离流形偏差来识别对抗样本。

Result: 在多种VLP架构和威胁设置（包括单模态攻击、多模态攻击以及自适应攻击）下，GeoDetect均能可靠地检测对抗样本，展现出鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: GeoDetect通过利用嵌入空间的几何特性，为提升VLPs的安全性和可靠性提供了一种鲁棒且实用的对抗检测方法。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-trained models (VLPs) are widely used in real-world applications. However, they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Although adversarial detection methods have demonstrated success in single-modality settings (either vision or language), their effectiveness and reliability in multimodal models such as VLPs remain largely unexplored. In this work, we study the geometry of VLP embedding spaces and observe structured anisotropy that differs from unimodal vision models. Our theoretical analysis shows that under this anisotropic structure, adversarial attacks increase the expected geometric separation between clean and adversarial examples (AEs). Specifically, we demonstrate that AEs consistently exhibit greater expected distances to randomly sampled points than their clean counterparts, indicating that AEs tend to push representations out of manifold regions. Building on these insights, we propose GeoDetect, which leverages these off-manifold deviations via geometric scores to identify AEs. Through comprehensive evaluations, we show that our approach reliably detects AEs across diverse VLP architectures and threat settings, covering unimodal and multimodal attacks as well as adaptive attacks, thereby providing a robust and practical approach to improving the safety and reliability of these models.

</details>


### [46] [FoMoVLA: Bridging Visual Foresight and Motion Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14739)
*Wei Li,Peijin Jia,Yuan Ma,Xuefeng Jiang,Titong Jiang,Sheng Sun,Yujian Li,Xin Wen,Han Hong,Zhikang Liu,Bailin Li,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoMoVLA通过联合学习未来特征预测和稀疏2D点跟踪增强VLA模型的状态估计和运动引导，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能和零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型本质上是反应式的，缺乏对世界动态的前向预测；而视觉预见方法虽能预测未来状态却缺少明确的运动引导，无法给出"如何到达"的连续运动路径。

Method: 提出FoMoVLA框架：1) 引入紧凑预见性令牌解码未来特征状态；2) 解码稀疏时序2D点轨迹建模几何运动；3) 通过轻量级未来条件交叉注意力模块耦合二者，实现预期状态与点动力学之间的一致性推理。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop和LIBERO-Plus数据集上达到SOTA性能，并展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 未来特征预测和稀疏点跟踪是互补的：前者提供目标状态，后者捕获连续运动路径；二者联合增强连续动作策略显式时空监督，有效提升VLA模型能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved impressive results in visuomotor policy learning, yet remain fundamentally reactive, mapping current observations and language to actions without explicit forward prediction of world dynamics. Existing visual foresight methods predict future visual states but lack explicit motion guidance: they show where to go but not how to get there. We argue that future feature prediction and sparse point tracking are naturally complementary: the former provides the goal state, while the latter captures the continuous motion path toward it. We propose FoMoVLA, a framework that augments VLA representations with explicit spatio-temporal supervision by jointly learning future feature foresight and sparse 2D point tracking, enhancing the continuous action policy. FoMoVLA introduces compact foresight tokens to decode future feature states, decodes sparse temporal 2D point trajectories to model compact geometric motion, and couples both through a lightweight future-conditioned cross-attention module that enables consistent reasoning between anticipated states and point dynamics. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa GR-1 Tabletop, and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and strong zero-shot generalization. Project page is available at https://liauto-research.github.io/FoMoVLA.

</details>


### [47] [On the Disagreement in Perturbation-based xAI -- Benchmarking Perturbation Choices for Flood Detection from SAR Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14743)
*Anastasia Schlegel,Ronny Hänsch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于扰动的可解释人工智能（xAI）方法中两个关键参数——补丁几何（大小和形状）和扰动类型（替换方案）对相关性估计的影响，并以SAR影像洪水检测为用例，发现不同参数设置会导致相关性图出现模糊甚至矛盾的解释，强调了方法设置的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扰动的xAI方法虽然被广泛使用，但对参数选择高度敏感，缺乏系统性的参数影响分析，可能导致不可靠的解释结果。

Method: 以合成孔径雷达（SAR）影像洪水检测为用例，系统改变扰动管道中的补丁几何（大小、形状）和扰动类型（替换方案），通过视觉检查、跨策略一致性评估以及对模型推理的忠实度度量，分析不同设置下相关性估计的变化。

Result: 不同扰动参数选择会引导生成不同的相关性图，产生模糊甚至相互矛盾的解释，表明参数设置对解释结果有显著影响。

Conclusion: 扰动方法中的参数设置是影响解释结果的关键因素，需要仔细检查和评估扰动选择，并将其视为解释过程中不可分割的一部分，以确保对解释和模型预测的稳健理解。

Abstract: Perturbation-based xAI methods are widely used to analyze the behavior and predictions of deep learning models. By altering input regions and measuring the resulting changes in class probabilities with respect to the original image, they assign relevance scores and generate heatmaps that reflect each region's contribution to the prediction. Despite their apparent simplicity, however, perturbation-based methods are sensitive to parameter choices. In this work, we focus on two key parameters of the perturbation pipeline, namely the patch geometry, including the size and shape of the perturbed regions, and the perturbation type, defined by the replacement scheme. Grounded in the use case of flood detection from Synthetic Aperture Radar imagery, we conduct a comprehensive investigation of how relevance estimation changes under different perturbation settings. Beyond visual inspection of the resulting relevance maps, we evaluate their consistency across perturbation strategies and their faithfulness to the model's reasoning. We demonstrate how different perturbation choices can steer the resulting relevance maps, yielding ambiguous and even contradictory explanations. Our findings emphasize the importance of methodological settings in perturbation-based xAI. They underscore the need to carefully inspect and evaluate perturbation choices and to treat them as an integral part when interpreting explanations, ensuring a robust understanding of both the explanations and model predictions.

</details>


### [48] [Clean-Reference Streaming Detection of Lens Occlusion and Photometric Transitions for Camera Tamper Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14760)
*Bo Ma,WeiQi Yan,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对监控摄像头的流式完整性监测器，通过比较亮度/梯度统计量与滑动参考、粗网格结构光抑制和快速亮度抑制，检测纹理塌陷遮挡和光度突变两种传感器故障，在受控序列上达到0.800 F1和0.822平衡准确率，在公共审计中实现0.925召回率@0.025误报率，并保持零误报的已验证阴性记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控摄像头的物理退化（如镜头遮挡、光照突变）会无声地破坏下游数据消费，而现有方案需要低误报率、有限计算和可诊断行为，特别是在干扰光照变化条件下。

Method: 设计了有限状态机，通过采样亮度/局部梯度统计与干净滑动参考比较，结合粗网格结构光抑制和模式/快速亮度抑制机制，每次篡改事件至多发出一次通知；形式化了决策谓词并推导了快速亮度抑制使场景转换路径不可达的一致性规则。

Result: 在320个受控序列上：F1=0.800，平衡准确率=0.822；公共审计中：5%误报预算下部分AUC最高，扩展应力测试中0.925召回率@0.025误报率；9.09相机小时公共审计零误报；UHCTD范围内召回率0.667 vs 范围外0.016。

Conclusion: 该方法应被解释为可审计的传感器健康子系统而非通用相机篡改分类器，在限定的故障签名范围内表现可靠。

Abstract: A surveillance camera is an image sensor whose silent physical degradation invalidates every downstream consumer of its data. In-situ integrity alarms for such vision sensors require low false-alarm rates, bounded computation, and diagnosable behavior under nuisance illumination changes. This paper studies a deliberately narrow streaming integrity monitor for two low-cost sensor-fault signatures: texture-collapsing lens occlusion and abrupt photometric scene transition. The detector compares sampled luminance and local-gradient statistics with a clean-only sliding reference, applies coarse-grid structured-light rejection and mode/rapid-brightness suppression, and emits at most one notification per tamper episode. We formalize the decision predicates and derive a consistency rule for when rapid-brightness suppression makes the scene-transition path unreachable. On 320 in-scope controlled sequences, the default state machine attains 0.800 F1 and 0.822 balanced accuracy (significantly better paired correctness than the strongest baseline, though the F1 margin is not statistically resolved); on a magnitude-swept public audit it attains the highest partial AUC under a 5\% false-alarm budget, and a separate extended-stress FPR-constrained sweep reaches 0.925 recall at 0.025 false-positive rate. Public Xiph, Bremen IoT, and UHCTD diagnostics show the fixed predicates preserve low false alarms while recall concentrates inside the declared envelope (UHCTD in-scope covered recall 0.667 versus 0.016 out of scope), and a 9.09-camera-hour verified-negative public audit records zero false alarms. The method is best interpreted as an auditable sensor-health subsystem rather than a universal camera-tamper classifier.

</details>


### [49] [Rare Concept Generation via Counterfactual Inference in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14765)
*Zhengyuan Jiang,Haipeng Liu,Meng Wang,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实推断的扩散模型CI-Diff，用于解决稀有概念生成任务中模型固有常识偏差导致属性渲染错误问题，通过阻断常识偏差干扰并利用自然直接效应解耦不寻常属性，在RareBench基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在稀有概念生成任务中失败，原因是训练阶段固有的常识偏差导致物体与常见属性强关联，难以打破这种关联来生成具有不寻常属性的稀有概念。

Method: 提出CI-Diff方法：1) 阻断模型固有常识偏差的干扰；2) 利用自然直接效应（Natural Direct Effect）捕获文本提示对图像生成的独立影响；3) 重新表述无分类器引导机制以突出非典型属性。

Result: 在RareBench基准上的大量实验验证了CI-Diff优于当前最先进的扩散模型。

Conclusion: 首次将因果推断引入稀有概念生成任务，有效解决了常识偏差导致的属性错误渲染和概念组合不一致问题。

Abstract: Rare concept generation focuses on synthesizing customized images conditioned on text prompts that describe objects with unusual attributes. Previous works failed to align the generated images with rare concepts, resulting in incorrect attribute rendering or inconsistent composition of concepts. Such failures, as we observed, stem from the inherent common knowledge bias in the training stage of diffusion models, where objects are strongly associated with their common attributes, making it difficult to break these associations when generating rare concepts. To address such challenges, in this paper, we propose a novel Counterfactual Inference-based Diffusion approach, dubbed CI-Diff. CI-Diff blocks the interference of the model's inherent common knowledge bias and utilizes the Natural Direct Effect to capture the independent influence of the text prompt of rare concepts on image generation so that decoupling the unusual attributes from the rare concepts. To this end, we reformulate the classifier-free guidance mechanism to highlight the atypical attributes. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to introduce causal inference into the rare concept generation task. Extensive experiments on the RareBench benchmark validate the superiority of CI-Diff over state-of-the-art diffusion models. Our code can be accessed from https://github.com/200204jzy/CI-Diff.

</details>


### [50] [TAMF-VTON: Texture-Aware Mask-Free Virtual Try-On via High-Fidelity Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14807)
*Jie Wang,Qian He,Gaofeng He,Xiaogang Jin,Huamin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAMF-VTON 是一个无需掩码的虚拟试穿框架，通过 MoE 适配、频域监督和自适应数据预处理，支持多衣物组合的高保真纹理迁移，在消费级硬件上实现 15 秒内推理，具备商业部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的虚拟试穿方法受限于分割掩码依赖、细粒度纹理保留不足以及对任意多衣物组合的支持有限，难以在真实电商场景中部署。

Method: 提出统一生成流水线，包含三个关键组件：(1) 轻量级 MoE 适配方案实现高效微调，不破坏基模型的编辑能力；(2) 频域监督机制显式优化高频谱一致性以保持纹理 fidelity；(3) 自适应修复策略的数据预处理管线，模拟逆 VTON 过程生成高质量训练对。

Result: 在定量指标和感知质量上均优于 SOTA 方法；在 RTX 4090 上采用 INT4 量化后每张图像推理时间低于 15 秒。

Conclusion: TAMF-VTON 通过无掩码操作、灵活的多衣物组合、忠实纹理保留以及消费级硬件上的高效推理，为数字时尚场景的可扩展部署提供了商业可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based virtual try-on (VTON) methods remain limited by their reliance on segmentation masks, insufficient preservation of fine-grained textures, and limited support for arbitrary multi-garment compositions. Consequently, existing approaches still face significant challenges in real-world e-commerce deployment. We present TAMF-VTON, a texture-aware, mask-free framework that enables high-fidelity image synthesis under practical unconstrained conditions. Our method requires no human parsing or inpainting masks at inference time and supports diverse garment styles, categories, and quantities, enabling the simultaneous transfer of multiple items while preserving body structure and intricate texture details. This is achieved through a unified generative pipeline with three key components: (1) a lightweight Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) adaptation scheme that enables efficient fine-tuning without compromising the base model's general editing capabilities; (2) a frequency-domain supervision mechanism that explicitly optimizes high-frequency spectral consistency to preserve high-fidelity textures; and (3) a robust data curation pipeline employing an adaptive inpainting strategy to simulate the inverse VTON process for high-quality training pair generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both quantitative metrics and perceptual quality. Optimized for efficiency, the model achieves inference in under 15 seconds per image on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 with INT4 quantization. By combining mask-free operation, flexible multi-garment composition, faithful texture preservation, and efficient inference on consumer hardware, TAMF-VTON demonstrates a commercially viable solution for scalable deployment in real-world digital fashion scenarios. The project is available at https://www.style3d.ai/ai-photoshoot/virtual-clothing-try-on.

</details>


### [51] [An LLM-Based Automatic Sportscast Solution for Robot Soccer Matches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14809)
*Francesco Petri,Michele Brienza,Daniele Nardi,Domenico Daniele Bloisi,Aldo Gangemi,Vincenzo Suriani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向RoboCup足球比赛的神经符号实时解说系统，能从视频流中提取精确统计数据并生成流畅、无幻觉的自然语言解说，兼容不同尺寸的人形机器人，支持直播与赛后分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoboCup社区需要持续衡量研究进展，而比赛视频的视觉统计计算是追踪进化的重要方式。当前缺乏能够实时、全自动地从RoboCup比赛视频中生成统计数据和自然语言解说的系统。

Method: 采用神经符号（neuro-symbolic）架构：前端从视频流中提取精确的原始运动跟踪数据，后端通过符号推理将统计信息转化为自然语言叙述，实现无幻觉（hallucination-free）的解说生成。

Result: 系统能够在直播过程中和赛后分析中实时生成比赛统计数据和评论，并能够适应联赛中不同尺寸人形机器人同场竞技的新动态场景。

Conclusion: 本文证明了神经符号方法在机器人足球比赛实时解说中的有效性，弥补了原始运动跟踪与自然语言生成之间的鸿沟，能够为RoboCup社区提供自动化的比赛分析工具。

Abstract: RoboCup has always been a scenario to develop systems that solve real-world problems. Driven by the main goal of playing against the 2050 FIFA World Cup champions, the RoboCup Soccer leagues need to constantly measure how the research community is progressing. Computing visual statistics from match videos is a crucial way to track this evolution. To address this challenge, this paper introduces a fully autonomous, real-time sports commentator for RoboCup matches. By bridging the gap between raw kinematic tracking and natural language generation, our neuro-symbolic architecture extracts precise statistics from video streams and turns them into fluent, hallucination-free narration. The proposed system is capable of generating statistics and commentary both during live match streaming and in post-game analysis, easily adapting to the new dynamism of the league where different humanoid robots of different sizes share the field. Supplemental materials are available at https://lab-rococo-sapienza.github.io/MARIO/

</details>


### [52] [Blurring Modal Boundaries: A Unified Survey from Single- to Multi-Modal Person Re-ldentification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14821)
*Xiao Wang,Bing Wang,Bin Yang,Cuiqun Chen,Xin Xu,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了行人重识别（ReID）从单模态RGB向跨模态与多模态范式的转变，涵盖可见光-红外（VI-ReID）、文本-图像（TI-ReID）、草图（Sketch-ReID）及非视距（NLOS）ReID等任务，并提出了一个基于Transformer的VI-ReID基线框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模态RGB行人重识别受限于低光照、遮挡等环境挑战，亟需向跨模态和多模态方向发展以提升鲁棒性，但缺乏对该领域系统性综述和统一框架的研究。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，分类梳理了可见光-红外、文本-图像、草图、非视距ReID以及三光谱和多模态融合ReID等任务，并提出了一个基于Transformer的VI-ReID基线框架以捕获模态不变特征。

Result: 系统总结了各跨模态ReID任务的数据集、挑战和方法论；提出了一个有效捕获模态不变特征的Transformer基线框架；指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 跨模态与多模态ReID是克服传统单模态局限的关键路径，基于Transformer的框架在VI-ReID任务上展现了潜力，未来可在NLOS-ReID等新兴方向进一步探索。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) serves as a critical component in intelligent surveillance systems, aiming to match identities across disjoint camera networks. While traditional methods primarily rely on single-modal RGB imagery, they are often constrained by environmental challenges such as low illumination and occlusion. To overcome these limitations, the field is rapidly evolving toward cross-modal and multi-modal paradigms. This survey presents a comprehensive overview of this transition, systematically reviewing key cross-modal tasks including visible-infrared (VI-ReID), text-image (TI-ReID), sketch-based (Sketch-ReID), and the emerging Non-Line-of-Sight (NLOS) ReID, which extends perception beyond direct visibility. Furthermore, we examine tri-spectral and multi-modal fusion ReID, discussing how complementary information from diverse sensors enhances robustness. Beyond summarizing datasets, challenges, and methodologies, we propose a Transformer-based baseline framework for visible-infrared ReID, designed to effectively capture modality-invariant features. Finally, based on the current landscape, we outline several promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [53] [Physics-Informed Diffusion for Biomechanically Plausible 3D Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14836)
*Emanuele Colonna,Moises Diaz,Gennaro Vessio,Miguel Angel Ferrer,Giovanna Castellano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIDiffSign是一种物理信息引导的扩散模型，通过将生物力学约束（骨长一致性、有效关节角度、手指关节约束）融入Transformer编码器-解码器架构和训练目标中，在保持语义保真度的同时生成更真实、符合解剖学约束的连续3D骨骼手语动作，在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily基准上优于强扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成方法使用基于坐标的语义重建目标，将骨架视为非结构化向量，导致骨长漂移、关节角度违规和手指临时锁定等生物力学不合理问题，无法同时满足语义保真度和生物力学合理性两个约束。

Method: 提出PIDiffSign，采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构，解码器通过自适应零初始化的层归一化（Adaptive Zero-Initialized Layer Normalization）以扩散时间步为条件，并交叉关注词汇表示。关键创新包括：可微几何模块强制骨长一致性和生物有效关节角度；训练目标结合人类学、运动学、角度和手指关节约束；对比性词汇-姿态对齐损失；无分类器引导实现语义条件采样。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily基准上，相比强扩散基线，在姿态精度、关节角度正确性、分布真实性和反向翻译质量方面均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 物理信息引导的扩散模型能够同时改善手语生成的动作真实性和语义保真度，验证了将解剖学约束融入扩散生成过程的有效性。

Abstract: Sign language production, which generates continuous 3D skeletal motion from spoken language input, must simultaneously satisfy two constraints: semantic fidelity, so that a deaf viewer can recognize the intended sequence of glosses, and biomechanical plausibility, so that the generated skeleton respects anatomical constraints. Existing approaches optimize semantic reconstruction through coordinate-based objectives that treat the skeleton as an unstructured vector, thus allowing for bone length drift, joint angle violations, and temporarily locked fingers. We introduce PIDiffSign, a physics-informed diffusion model for gloss-to-pose translation that incorporates anatomical constraints into both the architecture and training objective. The model uses a Transformer encoder-decoder, where the decoder is conditioned on the diffusion time step through adaptive zero-initialized layer normalization and cross-attends to gloss representations. A differentiable geometry module enforces bone length consistency and biologically valid joint angles throughout generation. Training combines anthropomorphic, kinematic, angular, and finger-joint constraints with a contrastive gloss-pose alignment loss and classifier-free guidance for semantically conditioned sampling. Experiments on the PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily benchmarks show consistent improvements over a strong diffusion baseline in pose accuracy, joint-angle correctness, distributional realism, and back-translation quality. These results demonstrate that physics-informed diffusion improves both motion realism and semantic fidelity for sign language generation.

</details>


### [54] [Rotational Motion-Induced Error Compensation for Phase-Shifting Profilometry-Based Eye Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14876)
*Seong-Jin An,Sanghoon Jeon,Yatong An,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于旋转运动补偿的相移轮廓术框架，用于动态3D眼表重建。通过从图像运动线索中估计眼球旋转，补偿帧间旋转引起的像素失配和相移误差，并采用区域优化策略减少残余伪影。实验表明该方法能有效抑制运动引起的形变，提高重建精度，为沉浸式环境中的高精度眼动追踪奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式头戴显示器中眼动追踪需要高精度，但传统2D图像方法稳定性、精度和鲁棒性不足。3D眼表重建可提供更丰富的几何信息，相移轮廓术虽能实现密集精确的表面测量，但眼动旋转时帧间采集易产生运动误差。

Method: 提出旋转运动补偿框架：利用用户特定3D眼球模型在球坐标系中从图像运动线索估计相对眼球旋转；基于估计运动补偿帧间旋转导致的相机像素失配和相移误差；引入区域优化策略，独立调整不同眼部区域的补偿强度以减少残余伪影。

Result: 在旋转假眼非均匀运动实验中，该方法显著抑制了运动引起的形变，提高了重建精度。非球形刚体实验表明补偿原理不限于球形眼球几何。

Conclusion: 该研究为PSP在动态3D眼表重建中实现稳定、高精度重建提供了实用基础，有助于未来沉浸式环境中的高精度眼动追踪。

Abstract: With the proliferation of immersive Head-Mounted Displays (HMDs) for Virtual and Augmented Reality (VR/AR), reliable and high-precision eye tracking has become increasingly important. Conventional 2D image-based methods offer low system complexity but remain limited in stability, accuracy, and robustness. Three-dimensional ocular surface reconstruction can provide richer geomet-ric information, and structured light profilometry is particularly attractive because it enables dense and accurate surface measurement. However, Phase-Shifting Profilometry (PSP), which estimates phase from sequentially acquired fringe images, is highly susceptible to motion-induced errors when the eye rotates between frames. This study proposes a rotational motion compensation framework for PSP-based dynamic 3D eye reconstruction. Relative eye rotation is estimated from image-based motion cues using a user-specific 3D eye model in a spherical-coordinate domain. The estimated motion is then used to compensate for camera-pixel mismatch and phase-shift errors caused by inter-frame rotation. A region-wise optimization strategy is further introduced to reduce residual artifacts by inde-pendently refining the compensation strength in different ocular regions. Experiments with a rotating fake eye under non-uniform motion demonstrate that the proposed method substantially suppresses motion-induced deformation and improves reconstruction accuracy. An additional experiment with a non-spherical rigid object indicates that the compensation principle is not restricted to spherical eye geometry. These results establish a practical basis for stable PSP-based dynamic 3D eye reconstruction toward future high-precision eye tracking in immersive environments.

</details>


### [55] [Selectivity Drives Efficiency: Dataset Pruning for Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14897)
*Tong Jin,Yunpeng Liu,Shuyu Hu,Chun Yuan,Song Wang,Feng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视觉地点识别（VPR）的按地点数据集剪枝框架，通过引入地点内多样性（IPD）和地点间相似性（IPS）两个互补指标，以地点为基本剪枝单元而非单张图像，从而构建紧凑且信息丰富的核心集，在显著降低存储和训练成本的同时保持高识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VPR研究依赖大规模数据集以提升性能，但带来了高昂的存储和训练成本，且传统数据集剪枝方法基于样本级分类范式，忽略了VPR中依赖图像对（而非独立图像）进行训练的关系特性。

Method: 提出按地点剪枝框架，以每个地点（而非单张图像）为基本剪枝单元；设计两种互补指标——IPD（地点内多样性）和IPS（地点间相似性）——联合评估每个地点的训练价值，据此对所有地点排序并构建紧凑核心集。

Result: 在多种剪枝比例下一致优于现有DP基线方法，且降低了选择和训练成本。在将合并数据集（约GSV-Cities的3.5倍）剪枝至可比规模后，仅用NetVLAD即可在MSLS-val达到94.5% R@1，在Nordland达到97.0% R@1。

Conclusion: 本文提出的按地点剪枝策略有效解决了VPR中数据集剪枝的难点，证明了在显著压缩数据规模的同时仍能训练出鲁棒且具判别力的VPR模型的可行性。

Abstract: Recent visual place recognition (VPR) studies have increasingly relied on large-scale datasets to train more robust and discriminative models. Although this trend significantly improves recognition performance, it also introduces substantial storage and training costs, especially when new architectures or training strategies need to be repeatedly developed and evaluated. Dataset pruning (DP) provides a promising way to improve data efficiency by retaining only informative training data. However, conventional DP methods mainly follow the sample-wise classification paradigm, which overlooks the relation-dependent training nature of VPR, where supervision is typically formed by image pairs rather than independent images. To address this issue, we propose a place-wise dataset pruning framework tailored for VPR. Instead of pruning individual images, our method treats each place as the basic pruning unit and introduces two complementary novel metrics, i.e., intra-place diversity (IPD) and inter-place similarity (IPS), to evaluate the training value of each place. By jointly considering these two metrics, our method ranks all places and constructs a compact yet informative coreset, thereby allowing the pruned dataset to still support the training of robust and discriminative VPR models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art DP baselines under different pruning ratios while reducing selection and training costs. Moreover, by pruning a merged dataset roughly 3.5$\times$ the size of GSV-Cities to a comparable scale, our coreset maintains highly competitive performance, achieving 94.5\% R@1 on MSLS-val and 97.0\% R@1 on Nordland with only NetVLAD. Codes will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [56] [FlashDecoder: Real-Time Latent-to-Pixel Streaming Decoder with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14898)
*Minguk Kang,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashDecoder是一种快速、内存高效的纯Transformer视频解码器，通过逐帧解码和滚动KV缓存机制，在保持重建质量的同时，实现了比传统3D卷积解码器3.6-4.7倍的解码速度提升和高达11倍的内存节省，支持恒定延迟的流式解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在视频扩散模型依赖3D卷积解码器，在高分辨率或长视频场景下解码速度慢且内存消耗大，无法满足实时视频生成对快速解码的需求。

Method: 提出FlashDecoder纯Transformer解码器，(1) 逐帧解码latent到像素；(2) 当前帧仅通过滚动KV缓存关注固定大小的过去帧窗口；(3) 固定时间窗口确保解码速度和内存与视频长度无关，支持恒定延迟流式解码；(4) 顺序处理帧自动强制执行时间因果性，无需显式注意力掩码。

Result: 在Wan2.1和Wan2.2潜在空间上，1080p分辨率下PSNR为41.55dB vs 41.49dB（匹配卷积解码器）；解码速度提升3.6-4.7倍；内存节省高达11倍；结合架构感知推理优化后，加速比可达12倍。

Conclusion: FlashDecoder证明了纯Transformer架构在视频解码任务中可以兼顾重建质量和高效推理，通过滚动KV缓存机制实现了与视频长度无关的恒定延迟解码，为高分辨率实时视频生成提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time video generation demands fast decoding as much as fast denoising, yet current latent video diffusion models rely on 3D convolutional decoders that are slow and memory-intensive at high resolutions or for long video. We introduce FlashDecoder, a fast, memory-efficient pure-Transformer video decoder that decodes latents to pixels frame by frame. At each step, the current frame attends only to a fixed-size window of past frames through a rolling KV cache. The fixed temporal window keeps decoding fast and memory bounded regardless of video length, enabling constant-latency streaming. Because frames are processed sequentially, temporal causality is enforced without explicit attention masks, enabling training at resolutions up to 1080p and matching the reconstruction quality of convolutional decoders. On the Wan2.1 and Wan2.2 latent spaces, FlashDecoder matches each convolutional decoder in reconstruction quality (e.g., 41.55dB vs. 41.49dB PSNR at 1080p) while decoding 3.6x-4.7x faster with up to 11x less memory on a single H100 GPU. With architecture-aware inference optimizations, the speedup widens to 12x.

</details>


### [57] [TanGO: Training-Free 3D Editing via Tangent-Space Guidance and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14927)
*Siwoo Lim,Sunjae Yoon,Gwanhyeong Koo,Hyeonseo Yun,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TanGO提出了一种无需训练的三维生成编辑框架，通过在生成动力学的切空间中实现自适应逐令牌控制，利用单步最优控制规则和基于von Mises-Fisher的方向差异来确定每个令牌的控制强度，从而减少语义伪影并实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配三维生成模型（如VecSet）采用结构化表示，但其令牌共享全局上下文，导致传统的无需训练编辑方法出现语义伪影（如保留区域坍塌或变换不完整）。

Method: 提出TanGO框架，在生成动力学的切空间中实现自适应逐令牌控制；设计单步最优控制规则；利用基于von Mises-Fisher的源与目标速度场方向差异确定每个令牌的控制信号强度。

Result: TanGO显著减少了结构伪影，在三维编辑基线中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 提出的无需训练框架TanGO通过切空间自适应逐令牌控制有效解决了语义伪影问题，为三维生成编辑提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While recent flow-matching 3D generative models (e.g., VecSet) adopt structured representations, their tokens share global context, causing conventional training-free editing to suffer from semantic artifacts such as collapsed preserved regions or incomplete transformations. To address this, we propose TanGO, a training-free framework that enables adaptive per-token steering in the tangent space of generative dynamics. To realize this selective control, we formulate a one-step optimal control rule and determine the strength of each token's control signal using a von Mises-Fisher inspired directional discrepancy derived from the source and target velocity fields. Experiments show that TanGO substantially reduces structural artifacts and achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing 3D editing baselines. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/siw00-lim/TanGO.

</details>


### [58] [Benchmarking Face Recognition without Real Faces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14932)
*Paweł Borsukiewicz,Daniele Lunghi,Wendkûuni C. Ouédraogo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究合成人脸数据集能否替代真实数据集用于人脸识别模型的评估。通过测试12种合成数据集与7个真实基准、24个预训练模型，发现 MorphFace 和 Vec2Face 能可靠复现真实基准的相对行为，表明高质量合成数据可支持隐私保护的全合成评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管合成人脸数据已可用于训练高精度人脸识别模型，但评估环节仍依赖真实人脸基准数据集，这仅解决了隐私问题的一半。本文旨在验证合成数据集能否完全替代真实基准进行模型评估。

Method: 使用24个预训练模型（涵盖卷积和Transformer架构），在12个合成数据集与7个真实基准上系统比较，涵盖生物特征验证指标、相似度分数分布、跨模型排序一致性及数据分布属性。

Result: 合成数据集的基准保真度差异很大，但 MorphFace 和 Vec2Face 两个最强候选能可靠复现真实基准的相对行为，其一致性程度落在真实基准之间自然存在的差异范围内。

Conclusion: 精心构建的合成数据集可以为人脸识别提供可靠的比较评估，推动该领域走向完全基于合成数据、保护隐私的训练与评估流程。

Abstract: Synthetic face datasets have become effective enough to train face recognition models with accuracy rivaling that of models trained on real photographs. This progress sidesteps the ethical and legal burdens of collecting real biometric data, yet evaluation has not kept pace. Even studies that train entirely on synthetic images still rely on real-face benchmarks to measure performance, leaving the privacy problem only half solved. We ask whether synthetic datasets can replace real benchmarks for face recognition evaluation. We test 12 synthetic datasets against 7 established real benchmarks using 24 pre-trained models that span both convolutional and transformer architectures. Our evaluation covers biometric verification metrics, similarity score distributions, cross-model ranking consistency, and the underlying distributional properties of each dataset. Benchmarking fidelity varies widely across the synthetic candidates, but the two strongest, MorphFace and Vec2Face, reproduce the relative behavior of real benchmarks and reach agreement levels that fall within the natural disagreement already observed among the real benchmarks themselves. These results establish that well-constructed synthetic datasets can support reliable comparative evaluation for face recognition, moving the field closer to a fully synthetic and privacy-preserving pipeline for both training and benchmarking.

</details>


### [59] [Still image and spatial-temporal tomato data enabling detection, segmentation, tracking, and video-instance segmentation using strong and weak labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14934)
*Michael Halstead,Esra Guclu,Mohamed Farag,Enrico Pallotta,Christian Hund,Ribana Roscher,Maren Bennewitz,Juergen Gall,Cyrill Stachniss,Chris McCool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了两个番茄植株视觉数据集：BUTom21（静态图像+人工标注）和BUTom-ST21（视频数据+AI半自动标注），均提供像素级果实成熟度标签，旨在为田间表型研究提供真实场景的挑战性数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究社区提供在商业化种植环境下获取的、具有挑战性的真实世界影像数据，以探索番茄植株及其果实的感知与状态估计方法。

Method: 构建两个数据集：BUTom21使用静态图像和人工标注；BUTom-ST21使用视频数据和基于AI方法的半自动标注（伪标签），均提供像素级果实成熟度标注。

Result: 发布了两个涵盖像素级成熟度标注的数据集，其中BUTom-ST21包含时空信息，可提供单个果实计数和成熟度信息。

Conclusion: 这些数据集为田间表型研究提供了真实场景下的挑战性基准，有助于推动基于视觉的番茄感知和表型分析方法的边界拓展。

Abstract: In this manuscript we release two datasets for visual sensing of tomato plants grown in commercial-like settings and acquired using a robot. The first is BUTom21 which consists of still images and manual annotations. The second is BUTom-ST21 which consists of video-based data and semi-automated annotations through AI-based methods, referred to as pseudo-labels. In both cases, we provide pixel-level labels for the ripeness of the fruit. The aim is to provide the research community a challenging set of real-world imagery to explore methods to sense and estimate the state of tomato plants and their fruit, which is an important horticultural crop. Importantly, the spatial-temporal dataset provides individual fruit count and ripeness information enabling researchers to push the boundaries of field-based phenotyping.

</details>


### [60] [VideoChat3: Fully Open Video MLLM for Efficient and Generalist Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14935)
*Xinhao Li,Yuhan Zhu,Xiangyu Zeng,Yuhao Dong,Haoning Wu,Zhiqiu Zhang,Yuandong Yang,Changlian Ma,Qingyu Zhang,Yansong Shi,Xinyu Chen,Haoran Chen,Zizheng Huang,Jun Zhang,Kun Ouyang,Lin Sui,Ziang Yan,Yicheng Xu,Chenting Wang,Yinan He,Hongjie Zhang,Yi Wang,Yu Qiao,Yali Wang,Ziwei Liu,Kai Chen,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoChat3是一个完全开源的高效通用视频多模态大模型，通过I3D-ViT和自适应帧分辨率实现高效时空表示，并利用可扩展视频数据合成流水线构建多样化训练数据集，以4B参数超越同等或更大规模的开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源视频理解模型存在三个主要问题：难以泛化到多种视频类型、计算成本高、以及关键组件（训练代码、策略、数据集）未完全开源导致可复现性差。为解决这些问题，作者提出了完全开源、高效且通用的VideoChat3。

Method: VideoChat3采用两个互补设计：1) 效率方面，提出膨胀3D视觉Transformer（I3D-ViT）和自适应帧分辨率用于流式视频感知，实现高效时空表示并降低视频输入处理成本；2) 效果方面，开发可扩展的视频数据合成流水线，构建三个多样化高质量训练数据集（VideoChat3-Academic2M、VideoChat3-LV116K、VideoChat3-OL617K），覆盖通用、长视频和流式视频场景。

Result: 在通用、长视频和流式视频基准测试中，VideoChat3以仅4B参数且更高效率超越同等或更大参数规模的开源模型。

Conclusion: VideoChat3实现了广泛的泛化能力和计算效率之间的罕见平衡，通过完全开源的设计推动社区发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in video understanding have spanned motion, long video, and streaming interaction, driving this field toward real-world applications. Despite this progress, current open-source models remain limited in several ways. They often struggle to generalize across diverse video types, making them effective only in specific domains. High computational demands further restrict their efficiency and scalability. Moreover, most models are only partially open, with key components such as training code, strategy, or datasets unavailable, which hinders reproducibility and slows community-driven development. To address these issues, we introduce VideoChat3, a fully open, efficient, and generalist video-centric MLLM. VideoChat3 advances video understanding through two complementary designs. For efficiency, we introduce Inflated 3D Vision Transformer (I3D-ViT) and Adaptive Frame Resolution for Streaming Video Perception, which enables efficient spatiotemporal representation and reduces the cost of processing video inputs during training and inference. For effectiveness, we develop a scalable video data synthesis pipeline that curates three diverse, high-quality training datasets: VideoChat3-Academic2M, VideoChat3-LV116K, and VideoChat3-OL617K, covering general, long-form, and streaming video scenarios, improving the model's generalization across domains. By integrating these designs, VideoChat3 achieves a rare balance of broad generalization and computational efficiency. Experiments across general, long-form, and streaming benchmarks demonstrate that VideoChat3 surpasses prior open-source models with equal or larger parameter counts with only 4B parameters and higher efficiency.

</details>


### [61] [Frequency-Structured Field Learning for Light-Field Disparity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14941)
*Sara Monji-Azad,Yulin Liu,Jürgen Hesser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqLF，一种基于EPI引导的傅里叶-局部潜场学习框架，无需显式构建视差体，通过全局傅里叶更新与局部卷积结合，在光场视差估计任务上接近强监督基线方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光场视差估计需要在平滑区域保持全局一致性，同时在遮挡边界、薄结构和深度突变的区域保持局部精度。现有方法依赖局部窗口、离散视差假设、大内存消耗的代价体或注意力聚合，存在效率或精度瓶颈。

Method: FreqLF从水平和垂直EPI堆栈中编码角度视差线索，并结合中心视图外观特征，投影到潜场后通过堆叠的混合傅里叶-局部层进行更新。傅里叶低模态更新实现全局特征交互，局部卷积保持空间变化，最后用坐标条件的高斯混合解码器预测视差。

Result: 在HCI 4D Light Field Benchmark上，FreqLF接近强监督基线方法的精度，同时基础模型避免显式代价体构建。消融实验证实傅里叶和局部分支的互补作用，缩放实验展示不同空间分辨率下的实用行为。

Conclusion: 傅里叶-局部潜场学习是光场视差估计的一个有竞争力的新范式，为未来研究提供了替代方向。

Abstract: Light-field disparity estimation requires global consistency in smooth or textureless regions and local precision near occlusion boundaries, thin structures, and abrupt depth transitions. Existing methods address these requirements through EPI matching, cost-volume or focal-stack construction, view aggregation, or direct convolutional regression, often relying on local windows, discrete disparity hypotheses, memory-intensive volumes, or attention-based aggregation. We instead formulate disparity estimation at the field level, predicting disparity from globally and locally updated EPI-derived latent features without explicitly constructing a disparity volume. We introduce FreqLF, an EPI-guided Fourier-local framework that encodes angular parallax cues from horizontal and vertical EPI stacks together with central-view appearance features. These cues are projected into a latent field and updated through stacked hybrid Fourier-local layers. Fourier low-mode updates enable global feature interaction, while local convolutions preserve spatial variations needed for fine disparity detail. A coordinate-conditioned Gaussian-mixture decoder then predicts disparity, using the mixture mean as the final estimate. Experiments on the HCI 4D Light Field Benchmark show that FreqLF approaches the accuracy of strong supervised baselines while avoiding explicit cost-volume construction in the base model. Ablations confirm the complementary roles of the Fourier and local branches, and scaling experiments demonstrate practical behavior across spatial resolutions. These results suggest that Fourier-local latent field learning is a competitive alternative for light-field disparity estimation. The code will be published soon.

</details>


### [62] [Introspective Attention Modulation for Safe Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14945)
*Basim Azam,Hossein Rahmani,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于推理时注意力动态调控的安全文本到图像生成方法，通过分析并重新平衡注意力激活来引导生成远离不安全概念，同时保持语义对齐，在安全性和生成质量上均优于现有概念擦除方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的T2I模型虽具有强大的生成能力，但仍易生成不安全内容。先前的方法如概念擦除、提示过滤或基于分类器的门控机制容易被简单的参数高效微调手段绕过，缺乏内在鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种推理时内省（inference-time introspection）方法，在图像合成过程中动态分析和重新平衡注意力激活（attention activations），通过调控注意力空间引导生成远离不安全概念，同时保持语义对齐。

Result: 在标准安全基准和对抗性安全基准上，该方法取得了卓越的安全分数，同时保持甚至提升了语义对齐和感知质量。实验表明注意力空间调控比现有概念擦除机制更有前景。

Conclusion: 注意力空间调控为扩散Transformer的图像生成安全提供了一条更优路径，模型代码已开源。

Abstract: State-of-the-art flow based text-to-image (T2I) models exhibit remarkable generative abilities but remain vulnerable to producing unsafe content. Prior safety efforts range from concept erasure and prompt filtering to classifier-based gating. However, simple techniques like parameter efficient adaptations of the models easily bypass such guardrails. We introduce a unique principled approach that achieves safety by regulating the model's attention dynamics through inference-time introspection, exhibiting intrinsic robustness. Our method analyzes and rebalances attention activations throughout image synthesis, steering generations away from unsafe concepts while preserving semantic alignment. This introspective control ensures safety of deployed models. Across standard and adversarial safety benchmarks, our approach achieves remarkable safety scores while maintaining or even improving alignment and perceptual quality. Our results reveal that attention-space regulation offers a considerably more promising path to safer diffusion transformer based image generation than the existing concept erasing mechanism.Our code can be accessed at https://basim-azam.github.io/iam/

</details>


### [63] [DINE: Distance Is Not Enough -- Learning Global Deformation Priors for Robust Soft-Tissue Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14946)
*Sara Monji-Azad,Rohit Beer,Marvin Kinz,Claudia Scherl,Jürgen Hesser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINE是一个最大后验框架，通过学习位移向量场的统计先验来增强非刚性点云配准，在DeformedTissue和SynBench数据集上显著降低了Chamfer距离并提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的非刚性点云配准方法大多依赖局部目标函数（如Chamfer距离），仅约束逐点邻近性，未考虑全局形变场的合理性，导致在大变形、噪声和离群点下对应估计困难。

Method: 提出DINE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段用Chamfer距离训练配准主干网络；第二阶段用第一阶段的形变场估计统计先验（全场PCA高斯先验或逐向量归一化流先验），再结合距离项与负对数先验项联合优化。应用于Robust-DefReg和DefTransNet两个主干网络。

Result: 在DeformedTissue上，DINE-PCA相比单阶段主干在不同形变等级下Chamfer距离降低约27-69%，对离群点鲁棒性提升66%，对高斯噪声鲁棒性提升83%。在SynBench上，从中等到大形变等级改善约59-79%。

Conclusion: 全局形变合理性是可靠软组织点云配准的重要约束条件，DINE框架通过引入形变先验有效提升了配准精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Non-rigid point cloud registration is central to soft-tissue shape analysis, but large deformations, noise, and outliers make correspondence estimation challenging. Most learning-based methods rely on local objectives such as Chamfer distance, which encourage point-wise proximity but do not constrain the global plausibility of the predicted deformation field. We address this limitation with DINE, a maximum a posteriori framework that augments distance-based registration with a learned statistical prior over displacement vector fields. DINE is applied to two registration backbones, Robust-DefReg and DefTransNet, using a two-stage strategy: a first-stage model is trained with Chamfer distance, its predicted deformation fields are used to estimate a prior, and the model is then refined with a combined distance and negative log-prior objective. We compare a full-field PCA Gaussian prior with a per-vector normalizing-flow prior. Experiments on DeformedTissue and SynBench show lower mean Chamfer distance under deformation and corruption. On DeformedTissue, DINE-PCA reduces Chamfer distance by approximately 27--69\% relative to the corresponding Stage-1 backbone across deformation levels, and improves robustness by up to 66\% for outliers and 83\% for Gaussian noise. On SynBench, improvements are modest at the smallest deformation levels and reach approximately 59--79\% from moderate to severe deformation. These results suggest that global deformation plausibility is an important constraint for reliable soft-tissue point cloud registration. (The code will be published soon.)

</details>


### [64] [U-shaped Multi-granularity Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14966)
*Biao Chen,Yunqian Yu,Xiangxu Zhao,Zhongshu Chen,Mengmeng Jing,Lin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UPrompt，一种U形多粒度提示学习框架，通过跨粒度级联增强与层次监督，解决视觉语言模型在密集预测任务中的粒度困境，在17个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示学习方法存在粒度困境：全局提示缺乏细粒度语义感知，局部提示忽略上下文关联，限制了跨任务泛化能力，尤其在密集预测任务中表现突出。

Method: 受U-Net启发，在视觉和文本模态中构建并行多粒度表示。通过粗到细的级联增强（全局上下文传播优化局部细节）和细到粗的层次监督（确保跨尺度语义一致性），实现多粒度信息的统一融合。

Result: 在17个基准上验证有效性。MSCOCO上超越MAMET和VPKE分别4.1和7.3 rSum；base-to-novel泛化超越CoCoA-Mix 5.09%；粗粒度开销极小，中粒度以1/3成本达到PSRC性能。

Conclusion: UPrompt通过U形多粒度提示学习框架有效解决了粒度困境，在不同粒度上均实现了性能与开销的良好平衡，证明了跨粒度融合对视觉语言模型密集预测任务的重要性。

Abstract: The prompt learning paradigm for vision-language models is effective yet faces a granularity dilemma: global prompts lack fine-grained semantic awareness, while local prompts ignore contextual associations, limiting cross-task generalization. This dilemma exists in dense prediction tasks. Inspired by U-Net, which unifies multi-level representations across granularities, we propose UPrompt, a U-shaped multi-granularity prompt learning framework for vision-language models. Similar to how U-Net integrates fine and coarse features through symmetric encoder-decoder pathways with cross-level connections, UPrompt constructs parallel multi-granularity representations in both visual and textual modalities, where coarse-to-fine cascaded enhancement propagates global context to refine local details, while fine-to-coarse hierarchical supervision ensures semantic consistency across scales. Extensive experiments on 17 benchmarks validate our effectiveness. UPrompt outperforms MAMET and VPKE by 4.1 and 7.3 rSum on MSCOCO, surpasses CoCoA-Mix by 5.09% in base-to-novel generalization, while maintaining competitive performance with minimal overhead (coarse-grained) and matching PSRC with 1/3 cost (medium-grained).

</details>


### [65] [Stitch-Inferencer: Enhance Endoscopic Video Segmentation and Tracking via Panoramic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14968)
*Shunsuke Kikuchi,Atsushi Kouno,Hiroki Matsuzaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stitch-Inferencer是一种实时、模型无关的推理框架，通过将跨帧的有效观测拼接成显式的全景图像空间画布，代替隐式的特征记忆，从而扩展内窥镜视野、缓解遮挡问题，在不重新训练的情况下提升下游分割和跟踪模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜感知受限于视野狭窄和器械频繁遮挡，现有视频模型依赖隐式特征记忆来存储时序上下文，但需要特定任务的视频训练、大量标注数据和更高的计算成本。

Method: 提出Stitch-Inferencer框架，将跨帧的有效观测像素在线拼接成显式的全景画布，代替隐式特征记忆；下游模型在拼接后的感兴趣区域上执行分割或跟踪，再将预测结果重投影回当前帧。

Result: 在解剖分割和点/框跟踪任务上，多种基线模型均获得一致改进，同时保持实时吞吐量；拼接模块单独运行速度超过60 FPS。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种实用的推理时解决方案，在计算受限的术中环境中增强内窥镜感知能力，且无需重新训练现有模型。

Abstract: Surgical video understanding is fundamental to navigation systems. Endoscopic perception is often hindered by a limited field-of-view and frequent instrument occlusions, making spatio-temporal context essential for robust inference. These challenges have motivated video models that aggregate information across frames. However, existing video models typically store past observations implicitly in learned feature representations, often requiring task-specific video training, substantial annotated data, and increased computational cost. We propose Stitch-Inferencer, a real-time, model-agnostic inference framework that replaces implicit feature memory with an explicit image-space panoramic canvas. By stitching valid observations across frames, Stitch-Inferencer preserves previously observed pixels in an online, instrument-free view, expanding the effective field-of-view and providing direct access to regions that are temporarily occluded or absent from the current frame. Downstream segmentation or tracking models are applied to a compact region of interest on the panorama, and their predictions are reprojected to the current frame, enabling existing models to exploit long-range context without retraining. Experiments on anatomy segmentation and point/box tracking demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse baselines while preserving real-time throughput. The stitching module alone runs at over 60 FPS, providing a practical inference-time solution to enhance endoscopic perception in computationally constrained intraoperative environments. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [66] [On Success and Simplicity: A Second Look at Transferable Vision-Language Attack Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14974)
*Yuchen Ren,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Bo Yang,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SimVLA，一个简单但更有效的视觉-语言对抗攻击流水线，通过解决跨模态交互和过度操作中的三个被忽视问题，在可迁移性和效率上显著超越现有复杂方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言预训练模型对抗攻击方法存在复杂的损失函数或多阶段攻击流程，但本文发现这些复杂设计反而存在三个被忽视的问题（由不恰当的跨模态交互和过度操作导致），因此希望设计更简单但更有效的攻击方法。

Method: 识别出三个被忽视的问题并提出SimVLA流水线，核心包括基于领域知识的跨模态词汇识别等简单有效策略，摒弃复杂的损失函数和多阶段设计。

Result: 在Flickr30k文本-图像检索数据集上，SimVLA在R@1可迁移性上超越SOTA基线8.01%-14.71%，仅消耗约35.73%的时间和46.26%的最大VRAM。

Conclusion: 利用领域知识（如跨模态词汇识别）比盲目追求复杂操作更重要，SimVLA可作为未来扩展的简单而有效的骨干方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language Pre-training Models (VLPMs) are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Recent transferable attacks on VLPMs have followed a common pipeline with complicated loss functions or multi-stage text/image attacks. However, in this paper, we demonstrate that such a sophisticated attack pipeline can be simpler yet more successful. Specifically, we identify three previously overlooked issues caused by inappropriate cross-modal interactions and excessive operations. To address them, we propose the Simple Vision-Language Attack (SimVLA) pipeline, which observably improves transferability and efficiency. Experiments on four datasets and three downstream tasks validate the superiority of our pipeline. For instance, on Flickr30k text-image retrieval dataset, our SimVLA outperforms the SOTA baseline in R@1 transferability by 8.01\%-14.71\%, while consuming only about 35.73\% of the time and 46.26\% of the max VRAM. Overall, the superiority of our SimVLA highlights the importance of leveraging domain knowledge (e.g., our proposed cross-modal word identification), while blindly pursuing intricate operations (e.g, complex loss functions and redundant multi-stage designs) may even be harmful. We hope our SimVLA can serve as a simple yet effective backbone for future extensions. Code is available at https://github.com/RYC-98/SimVLA.

</details>


### [67] [From Draft to Draft-Free: One-Step Video Object Removal via Privileged Distillation and Fast Planting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14976)
*Zizhao Chen,Ping Wei,Guang Dai,Jingdong Wang,Mengmeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D2DF框架，通过教师模型多步精炼粗稿、Prior-Privileged Consistency Distillation蒸馏为一步模型，并引入Self-Guided Fast Planting模块消除对粗稿的依赖，实现视频物体移除的快速高质量一步生成（约1秒/视频）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体移除方法中，基于光流或注意力的传统方法易产生伪影且不自然，而扩散方法虽提升视觉质量但需多步去噪，效率低下。作者旨在解决质量与效率的权衡问题。

Method: D2DF框架包含三部分：(1) 教师模型通过多步去噪将低质量粗稿精炼为高保真视频；(2) 利用Prior-Privileged Consistency Distillation (PPCD) 将多步能力蒸馏为一步学生模型；(3) 引入基于Temporal Masked Transformer的Self-Guided Fast Planting (SGFP) 模块，在潜空间自主生成场景一致的伪粗稿，实现完全无粗稿依赖的一步生成。

Result: 在多项指标上达到最先进水平，超越传统方法和多步生成方法，单个视频去噪过程仅需约1秒。

Conclusion: D2DF框架通过蒸馏和自主生成伪粗稿的设计，在保持高质量的同时显著提升效率，为视频物体移除任务提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Video object removal is a fundamental yet challenging task in video editing. Despite recent progress, existing methods typically fall into two categories. Traditional approaches based on optical flow or attention mechanisms often introduce noticeable artifacts and yield unnatural results. In contrast, diffusion-based methods improve visual realism but demand multiple denoising steps, limiting their practicality. To address these issues, we propose From-Draft-to-Draft-Free (D2DF), a framework that distills the ability of transforming coarse drafts into refined videos into a one-step video generation model. Within D2DF, a teacher model is trained to refine low-quality removal results ("drafts") into high-fidelity videos by multiple steps. Then, through Prior-Privileged Consistency Distillation (PPCD), we distill this capability into a student model that performs one-step removal conditioned on the draft. To eliminate draft dependency, we introduce a Self-Guided Fast Planting (SGFP) module based on our Temporal Masked Transformer that autonomously generates scene-consistent pseudo-drafts in latent space, enabling a fully draft-free one-step model. Extensive experiments show that both draft-conditioned and draft-free versions achieve state-of-the-art performance on multiple metrics, surpassing traditional and multi-step generative methods in both quality and efficiency. The denoising process for a single video takes only about 1 second.

</details>


### [68] [JADE-GS: Joint Alternating Deblurring Guided by Events in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14990)
*Haoyu Fu,Jiafeng Huang,Yuchen Wang,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JADE-GS通过像素自适应路由门融合物理事件积分先验和学习的网络先验，并将2D恢复器与3D高斯溅射学生模型耦合在双向循环中，利用多视图一致渲染和物理重模糊约束来正则化恢复器，从而在事件相机运动模糊场景下实现高质量3D重建，在合成和真实基准上取得最佳感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机快速运动时，图像模糊破坏了三维模型恢复清晰场景所需的帧内运动信息，而事件相机以微秒级分辨率捕捉了这一信号。然而，将其转化为可靠的3D监督面临两个障碍：物理事件积分先验和学习网络先验在恢复上以相反方式失败；现有流水线仅单向运行，原始事件噪声或固定2D伪标签的偏差会未经修正地传递到几何结构中。

Method: 提出JADE-GS方法：1) 像素自适应路由门融合物理事件积分先验（保留边缘但累积漂移）和学习网络先验（恢复纹理但扭曲边界）；2) 将2D恢复器与3D高斯溅射学生模型耦合在双向循环中，通过分离的多视图一致渲染和基于物理的重模糊约束来正则化恢复器，将固定的预处理器转变为几何感知预测器。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上取得最佳感知质量，LPIPS和CLIP-IQA领先，PSNR和SSIM具有竞争力；在单个消费级GPU上训练约1小时，内存低于5GB，同时保持实时渲染。

Conclusion: JADE-GS通过融合互补先验和双向耦合架构，有效解决了事件相机引导的3D重建中先验失效和误差传递问题，实现了高质量、高效率的实时渲染3D重建。

Abstract: When a camera moves fast during exposure, blur destroys the intra-exposure motion a 3D model needs to recover the sharp scene, while event cameras capture exactly this signal at microsecond resolution. Turning them into reliable 3D supervision faces two obstacles. First, the two restoration priors fail in opposite ways: physics-based event-integration priors preserve edges but accumulate drift; learned networks recover texture but distort boundaries. Second, existing pipelines run in one direction only, so raw event noise or the biases of fixed 2D pseudo-labels pass uncorrected into the geometry. JADE-GS addresses both: a pixel-adaptive routing gate fuses the complementary priors, and the resulting 2D restorer is coupled to a 3D Gaussian Splatting student in a bidirectional loop, where detached, multi-view-consistent renders and a physics-based reblurring constraint regularize the restorer, turning a fixed preprocessor into a geometry-aware predictor. Across synthetic and real benchmarks, JADE-GS attains the best perceptual quality, leading LPIPS and CLIP-IQA on both benchmarks with competitive PSNR and SSIM, and trainsin about one hour under 5 GB on a single consumer GPU while preserving real-time rendering.

</details>


### [69] [Video = World + Event Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15038)
*Lianghua Huang,Zhi-Fan Wu,Yupeng Shi,Wei Wang,Mengyang Feng,Cheng Yu,Chen Liang,Junjie He,Chen-Wei Xie,Yu Liu,Jingren Zhou,Ang Wang,Bang Zhang,Baole Ai,Chongyang Zhong,Jinwei Qi,Kai Zhu,Pandeng Li,Peng Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Xinhua Cheng,Yitong Huang,Yun Zheng,Yuxiang Bao,Yuzheng Wang,Zhiwei Lin,Zoubin Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wan-Streamer v0.3 提出了一种新的视频交互框架，将视频视为"世界+事件流"，通过在大规模真实视频上进行通用预训练，学习预测世界在实时输入下的变化，并特化到实时全双工音视频交互任务中，实现低延迟的视觉-语言-动作多模态理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频交互模型缺乏统一的组织视角，将视频的静态上下文（世界）和动态变化（事件流）混为一谈，难以在大规模真实视频上进行通用预训练并泛化到多种实时下游任务。

Method: 提出"世界+事件流"双要素视频建模框架；世界代表视频中持久不变的上下文（环境、场景、主体、声学条件、语音特征等），事件流代表随时间变化的一切（场景变化、主体行为、语音等）。基于此设计通用预训练任务：给定世界和输入，实时预测世界如何移动、变化和响应。预训练能力可特化到全双工音视频交互等下游任务，模型采用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）映射方式，将多模态用户输入映射为语言形式的语音和行为动作。

Result: 保留了v0.2的操作指标：640×368视频分辨率、25 FPS帧率、160 ms流式单元、约200 ms模型端响应延迟、在350 ms双向网络预算下实现约550 ms总交互延迟。

Conclusion: Wan-Streamer v0.3通过"世界+事件流"的重新组织视角，为视频理解提供了一种通用预训练范式，在保持低延迟的同时实现了从大规模真实视频学习到实时交互任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present Wan-Streamer v0.3, which reframes our native-streaming interaction model under a single organizing view: a video is a world plus an event stream. The world is the persistent context in which a video unfolds, including the environment, scene, subjects, ambient acoustic conditions, voice characteristics, and other relatively stable conditions. The event stream is everything that changes over time within that world, including scene or environmental changes, subject behavior, speech, and other sounds. This yields a general-purpose pretraining task over large amounts of real video: given a world and incoming input, predict how the world moves, changes, and responds in real time. The resulting competence can be specialized to a broad family of real-time downstream tasks. We instantiate it on real-time full-duplex audio-visual interaction, where the event stream is the agent's speech together with free-form behavior. Functionally, the model's multimodal understanding process is vision-language-action-like: it maps multimodal user input to language-form speech and behavior actions. Wan-Streamer v0.3 preserves the v0.2 operating point: 640x368 video at 25 FPS, a 160 ms streaming unit, approximately 200 ms model-side response latency, and approximately 550 ms total interaction latency under a 350 ms bidirectional network budget.

</details>


### [70] [Weakly-Supervised RGB-D Salient Object Detection via SAM-driven Pseudo Annotation and State Space Interaction-based Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15041)
*Wenqi Si,Gongyang Li,Shixiang Shi,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督RGB-D显著目标检测方法，包括SAM驱动的伪标注生成（SAM-PAG）和基于状态空间交互的条件扩散模型（S²Diff），通过稀疏涂鸦标注生成精确的显著图，在七个数据集上优于现有弱监督方法，性能接近全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少RGB-D显著目标检测中像素级标注的繁重负担，解决涂鸦标注缺乏物体结构和细节信息导致显著图不准确的问题。

Method: 提出SAM-PAG利用SAM通过双分支结构和分割掩码一致性将稀疏涂鸦扩展为密集像素级伪标注；提出S²Diff采用扩散模型逐步去噪显著图，通过跨模态条件生成模块（频率融合+隐式-显式状态空间交互）获取全局条件特征，并通过上下文注入模块增强目标信息、抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在七个数据集上优于相关涂鸦监督方法，性能与全监督方法相当。

Conclusion: SAM-PAG与S²Diff的紧密协作有效解决了弱监督RGB-D SOD中稀疏标注的难题，实现了接近全监督的性能水平。

Abstract: Weakly-supervised RGB-D Salient Object Detection (SOD) is explored to reduce the heavy burden of pixel-level annotations. But scribble annotations lack the structure and details of objects, resulting in inaccurate saliency maps. In this paper, we propose a novel scribble-supervised RGB-D SOD method, consisting of a Segment Anything Model (SAM)-driven pseudo annotation generation method (\emph{SAM-PAG}) and a state space interaction-based conditional diffusion model (\emph{$S^2$Diff}). Specifically, SAM-PAG is tailored to address the issue of sparse supervision information. In SAM-PAG, we adopt the advanced SAM to expand sparse scribbles to dense pixel-level pseudo annotations through the dual-branch structure and the consistency of segmentation masks. In $S^2$Diff, we adopt the diffusion model to iteratively refine the noisy saliency maps with the guidance of conditional information, generating accurate saliency maps. Naturally, the core of our $S^2$Diff lies in the acquisition of conditional features and the denoising of saliency maps. For the former, we employ a cross-modal conditional generation module to interweave cross-modal features through frequency integration and implicit-explicit state space interaction, effectively achieving global conditional features. For the latter, we employ a context injection module to mitigate noise interference and to enhance object information with the conditional context. With the close cooperation of SAM-PAG and $S^2$Diff, our method outperforms relevant scribble-supervised methods and achieves competitive performance compared to fully-supervised methods on seven datasets. The code and results of our method are available at https://github.com/Switch457/WeakS2Diff_SOD.

</details>


### [71] [Parameter-efficient Prompt Tuning of Vision Foundation Model With Adaptive Focal Loss for Interpretable MCI Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15047)
*Javad Khoramdel,Farhad Hoseyni,Amirhossein Nikoofard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种参数高效的冻结DINOv2-Small架构，通过三个模态特定可学习提示令牌和共享交叉注意力机制实现MCI检测，结合MoCA自适应焦点损失处理边界模糊性，在MCI分类上达到0.641 F1和0.795 AUC，超越ResViT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCI作为阿尔茨海默病前驱阶段至关重要，但现有方法受限于数据稀缺、类别不平衡和诊断边界模糊性；传统混合架构计算成本高且空间可解释性仅为事后近似而非模型固有属性。

Method: 采用冻结DINOv2-Small骨干，通过三个模态特定可学习提示令牌进行参数高效微调（仅119万参数）；每个令牌作为查询与源图像块令牌进行共享交叉注意力，直接通过注意力图实现空间可解释性；任务条件嵌入经注意力模块融合以量化模态级重要性；引入MoCA自适应焦点损失，将连续认知分数整合到训练目标、损失调制和自适应样本加权中，严格泛化标准软标签方法。

Result: 在分层五折交叉验证下，MCI类F1达到0.641，AUC达到0.795，比计算量更大的ResViT基线在MCI类F1上高出0.110。

Conclusion: 提出的参数高效框架在数据稀缺和边界模糊条件下有效提升了MCI检测性能，同时通过架构固有方式实现空间可解释性，避免了额外计算开销。

Abstract: Mild Cognitive Impairment is a critical early stage of cognitive decline that frequently precedes Alzheimer's disease, yet its automated detection from neuropsychological drawing tests remains fundamentally constrained by data scarcity, class imbalance, and diagnostic ambiguity near clinical boundaries. Existing methodologies attempt to bypass these constraints using computationally expensive, fully fine-tuned hybrid architectures that relegate spatial explainability to a post-hoc approximation rather than an intrinsic model property. We propose a parameter-efficient framework utilizing frozen DINOv2-Small model adapted via three modality-specific learnable prompt tokens while Operating with 1.19 million trainable parameters, each token serves as a query in a shared cross-attention layer over the source image patch tokens. Crucially, spatial explainability is achieved directly through these attention maps; as a structural consequence of the architecture. Then task-conditioned embeddings fused via an attention module to quantify modality-level importance per subject. To handle boundary ambiguity, a MoCA-adapted focal loss introduced that integrates continuous cognitive scores into the training target, loss modulation, and adaptive sample weighting, strictly generalizing standard soft-label approaches. Under stratified five-fold cross-validation, the proposed architecture yields an MCI-class F1 of 0.641 and an AUC of 0.795, outperforming the computationally heavier ResViT baseline by 0.110 in MCI-class F1.

</details>


### [72] [RoGS: Adaptive Meshgrid Gaussian for Large-Scale Road Surface Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15048)
*Tianchen Deng,Zhiheng Feng,Wenhua Wu,Ziming Li,Siting Zhu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROADGS-T框架，基于自适应网格高斯表示实现鲁棒高效的大规模道路表面重建，通过2D高斯面片网格建模、道路结构感知自适应策略和轨迹一致性位姿优化，显著提升重建质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于网格的道路表面重建方法在大型驾驶场景中面临重建质量有限和优化成本高的问题，需要更高效且鲁棒的表示方法。

Method: 1) 采用2D高斯面片放置在网格上建模道路表面，显式存储颜色、语义和几何信息；2) 提出道路结构感知的自适应网格策略，在车道线、路边界等复杂区域分配更密的高斯面片，平坦区域保持紧凑表示；3) 设计基于轨迹一致性引导的位姿鲁棒优化策略，从多个邻近位姿估计局部表面先验并自适应加权位姿引导的高度正则化。

Result: 相比传统网格表示和3D高斯原语，所提方法更好地匹配道路薄表面特性，显著减少冗余原语和重叠，并在大规模驾驶场景中实现更优的重建质量。
